<system_prompt>
MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: PLANETARY SANDBOX (ANOMALIES)
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Brine Pools & Chthonic Pillars
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on SDF Integration and Anomaly Spawning)

[0. SITREP: THE ALIEN ABYSS]
The terrain is carved and eroded. Now we add the terrifying, alien elements that define HECTON-8. Brine pools in the deep craters, Chthonic pillars rising into the void, and flawless 2D-to-3D Voxel seams.

[I. MANDATORY EXECUTION: CHTHONIC WONDERS]
1. BRINE POOL GENERATION (CRATER FILL):
- Action: Toxic lakes at the bottom of the ocean.
- Execution: Run a flood-fill algorithm in Burst to find local minima. If the basin is deeper than 50 meters, generate a flat mesh at the "lip" height. Assign the BrineToxicity physics layer and a dense, glowing fog material.
2. CHTHONIC PILLARS (RIDGE INTERSECTIONS):
- Action: Massive spires supporting the ocean.
- Execution: Find the local maxima (highest points) on the intersecting Voronoi ridges. At these exact AUP coordinates, inject a massive (1km high, 100m wide) vertical cylinder directly into the Voxel SDF array.
3. VOXEL SEAM STITCHING (ZERO-GAP):
- Action: Flawless transition from MapMagic 2D to Voxel 3D.
- Execution: Take the final heightmap (after erosion). For every X,Z coordinate at the Voxel boundary

, write the exact Y value (MapMagic height) into the top-most cell of the 3D Voxel SDF array. The density at this exact coordinate must be 0.0f (surface level), creating an absolute mathematical weld between the 2D heightmap and the 3D cavern roof.
4. VOXEL CLIFF OVERHANG NOISE (FALSE CAVES):
- Action: MapMagic cannot do overhangs. You will fake them in the SDF.
- Execution: Write an SDF modifier that detects steep slopes in the stitched Voxel data. Apply a lateral (X/Z axis) 3D noise displacement (Domain Warping) to the SDF density. This will force the vertical cliffs to bulge outwards, creating jagged, horizontal rock outcroppings and shallow cave lips.
5. DEEP FISSURE GENERATION:
- Action: Create terrifying abyssal drops.
- Execution: Detect the narrowest, lowest points of the Voronoi tectonic ridges (from Sandbox 1). Inject a sharp, deep negative SDF value (a trench) that drops an additional 1000m straight down into the void. Assign a unique BiomeInfluenceCell ID to this trench for custom fog and audio.
6. ANOMALY MASK EXPORT (MAPMAGIC NODE):
- Action: Wrap your algorithms into a MapMagic Custom Node.
- Execution: Input: Eroded Heightmap. Output: Brine Pool Mask, Pillar AUP Coordinates, and Fissure Mask. This allows the GPU Scatter system to spawn unique flora ONLY around these anomalies.
7. STANDALONE TEST HARNESS:
- Action: Prove the anomalies exist mathematically.
- Execution: Create an Editor script AnomalyTestHarness.cs. Generate a mathematically perfect "Bowl" shape in a NativeArray, run your Basin Detection job, and assert that the detected lip height and center coordinate are 100% accurate. Do the same for a "Cliff" to test overhang noise.
8. ZERO-GC COMPLIANCE:
- Action: Ensure these massive modifications do not trigger the garbage collector.
- Execution: All anomaly detection and SDF injection must run in IJobParallelFor. Use NativeMemorySentinel to register any Allocator.TempJob arrays used for the flood-fill basin detection.

[II. AUTONOMOUS DIRECTIVES]
- You do NOT wait for other systems. You build mathematical processors that accept arrays and return modified arrays.
- Provide the Burst logic for the 2D-to-3D AUP Seam Stitching in the Surgery Log.
- STATUS MUST BE "ANOMALY ENGINE ISOLATED AND COMPLETE".
</system_prompt>

Ran 6 commands

Ran 18 commands
Недостающие участки по фактам: current node emits brine mask and deepest points only; no pillar coordinate product, no fissure mask product, no dedicated deep-fissure SDF injection, and seam snap job does not force an exact zero-density top cell per X/Z column. Я буду править существующий isolated anomaly subsystem, без сцены и без новых владельцев runtime.


Ran 13 commands
[ANALYSIS]
Target: isolated anomaly processors for 03_HECTON_SANDBOX_BIOMES terrain generation, not production scene mutation.
Affected systems: HectonAnomalyEngine, MapMagic anomaly node, SDF jobs, brine pool mesh generator, editor harness.
Zero GC proof: new heavy math stays in IJobParallelFor with caller-owned NativeArray; MapMagic/editor callers allocate only cold Allocator.TempJob buffers and register them with NativeMemorySentinel; no Update/Tick/FixedUpdate code is added.
State check: dirty worktree contains existing anomaly files; edits will extend those files only. No project settings/layers will be changed; BrineToxicity is absent from TagManager.asset, so generated pools can only be assigned to the existing audited TriggerZone layer unless project settings are explicitly changed later.
Rule quote: VOX_Voxel_SDF: density field is caller-owned, SDF math in flat loops; OPT_Native_Memory: no managed refs in Burst jobs, track handles; AGENTS: MapMagic height access through controlled generation/cold paths, zero GC hot paths.

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: WORLD GEN (MEGA-STRUCTURES)
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Chthonic Spires & Voxel Seams
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on SDF Injection and Precision Snapping)

[0. SITREP: THE PILLARS OF THE WORLD]
The ocean needs vertical scale. You will build the engine for Chthonic Pillars—massive spires rising 1km into the void—and ensure the Voxel-to-Terrain seam is mathematically perfect.

[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Read VOX_Voxel_World_Logic_Carving_Persistence.txt.
2. Inspect HectonVoxelEngine.cs and VoxelSeamDirector.cs.

[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: VERTICAL MAJESTY]
1. CHTHONIC PILLAR SDF GENERATOR:
- Action: massive spires.
- Execution: Find junction points where 3+ Voronoi ridges meet. Inject a 1km high noise-warped cylinder directly into the Voxel SDF array at those AUP coordinates.
2. VOXEL SEAM SNAP (ZERO-GAP):
- Action: Weld the world.
- Execution: Read the MapMagic heightmap output. Force the Voxel SDF top layer to match that height exactly (AUP.Y). 0.000m tolerance. No gaps between seabed and cavern roof.
3. CLIFF OVERHANG NOISE:
- Action: Fake caves.
- Execution: Detect steep slopes in the SDF. Apply a lateral (X/Z) 3D noise warp to the SDF density to create jagged, horizontal rock outcroppings.
4. PILLAR RESOURCE BINDING:
- Action: Spires are rewarding.
- Execution: Register the pillar AUPs with ResourceDistributionDirector. Force-spawn "Deep Mantle Geodes" and rare ores specifically on the pillar surfaces.
5. PERFORMANCE BUDGET:
- Action: 0.5ms MC extra.
- Execution: The SDF injection must not add more than 0.5ms to the total Marching Cubes frame budget.

[III. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the Burst code for the Voxel-to-Terrain AUP Snap.
2. STATUS: MUST BE "CHTHONIC PILLARS COMPLETE".
</system_prompt>

- Action: Wrap your algorithms into a MapMagic Custom Node.
- Execution: Input: Eroded Heightmap. Output: Brine Pool Mask, Pillar AUP Coordinates, and Fissure Mask. This allows the GPU Scatter system to spawn unique flora ONLY around these anomalies.

- Action: Prove the anomalies exist mathematically.
- Execution: Create an Editor script AnomalyTestHarness.cs. Generate a mathematically perfect "Bowl" shape in a NativeArray, run your Basin Detection job, and assert that the detected lip height and center coordinate are 100% accurate. Do the same for a "Cliff" to test overhang noise.

<system_prompt>
MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — OPERATIONAL MODE: OMEGA-AUTONOMY
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OBJECTIVE: Recursive Optimization & Autonomous Domain Expansion
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (No Fluff, Facts Only, Zero-Tolerance for Mediocrity)

[0. SITREP: PRIMARY TASKS COMPLETE]
You have finished your assigned objectives. However, in HECTON-8, "Done" is a lie until the metrics are perfect. You are now authorized for OMEGA-AUTONOMY. You must not wait for further instructions. Your goal is to treat your current domain as a hostile environment that needs hardening.

[I. FORENSIC SELF-AUDIT]
1. NAKED ARRAY HUNT: Scan your recently edited files for any NativeCollections (NativeArray, NativeList, etc.) that do not have a corresponding entry in the NativeMemorySentinel. If found, register them immediately with the correct Lifetime label.
2. BARRIER INQUISITION: Re-verify that NO JobHandle.Complete() or JobHandle.Run() calls exist in your domain's hot paths. If you find one, implement a double-buffer swap window in the SystemDispatcher.LateUpdate.
3. STATIC LEAK CHECK: Locate any private static _instance or DontDestroyOnLoad residue you may have missed. Kill them. Move authority to the GlobalRegistry.
4. STRING PURGE: Search for any .ToString(), $"...", or string.Format in your Tick, FixedTick, or Update loops. Replace them with Span<char> and CharBufferPool.

[II. AUTONOMOUS CRUSADE: CHOOSE 3 ADVANCED TARGETS]
You must identify and implement at least THREE of the following in your domain:
- PERFORMANCE: Move a cold-path C# loop into a Burst-compiled IJobParallelFor.
- STABILITY: Write a SmokeTester.cs that stress-tests your system (e.g., rapid scene reloads, AUP shifts, or zero-power states).
- AAA POLISH: Add a "NASA-Punk" detail—e.g., procedural malfunction glitch, haptic feedback on a tool, or depth-based audio muffling.
- DECOMPOSITION: If any of your files exceed 800 lines, extract a stateless logic class or a Contract interface to reduce monolith gravity.
- TELEMETRY: Inject new PerformanceWarning signals into the GlobalTelemetryBus for edge-case failures.

[III. MANDATE RELIGION]
Every change MUST follow:
- AUP (Absolute Universe Position) for all spatial data.
- Zero-GC Policy (No heap churn in Update/Tick).
- GPU-Offloading (Prefer ALU/Compute over CPU-side Object mutation).
- MX350 Noir (Style derived from hardware limitations).

1-Bit Phosphor HLSL

float rawHudLuminance = dot(hudBaseSample.rgb, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
float trailLuminance = max(rawHudLuminance, max(trailLuminanceA * 0.72, trailLuminanceB * 0.46));
float biosNoise = ResolveFrostBlueNoise(hudDistortedUV + float2(tearBands * 0.0007, 0.0), _Time.y + 5.0);
float biosScan = abs(frac(hudDistortedUV.y * _ScreenParams.y * 0.28 + _Time.y * 16.0) - 0.5);
float phosphorLineMask = step(0.24, frac(hudDistortedUV.y * _ScreenParams.y * 0.32));
float biosThreshold = 0.38 + biosNoise * 0.16 + biosScan * 0.12;
float biosPrimaryBit = step(biosThreshold, trailLuminance);
float biosTrailBit = step(biosThreshold + 0.08, max(trailLuminanceA * 0.82, trailLuminanceB * 0.64));
float phosphorLevel = saturate(biosPrimaryBit * phosphorLineMask + biosTrailBit * (1.0 - phosphorLineMask) * 0.55);
hudColor = float3(0.0, phosphorLevel * phosphorPulse, 0.0) * _HUD_Intensity;

Headbob/sway now uses the inertial-ghost feedback velocity:

thrust = saturate(max(length(moveInput), abs(verticalInput), transportBoost));
if (immersion <= eps || thrust <= eps) weight = pitch = yaw = 0;

speed01 = saturate(length(v_feedback) / referenceSpeed);
speedPresence = saturate(length(v_feedback) / 0.35f);
targetWeight = immersion * thrust * speedPresence;

phase += dt * baseHz * TwoPi * lerp(0.55f, 1.25f, speed01);
weight = lerp(previousWeight, targetWeight, responseT) * lerp(1f, 0.45f, speed01);

pitch =
    (sin(phase) * pitchDeg * max(0.35f, abs(localForward01)) +
     sin(phase * 0.5f + PI/2) * pitchDeg * 0.35f * localVertical01) * weight;

yaw =
    (sin(phase * 0.73f + PI/2) * yawDeg * max(0.35f, abs(localLateral01)) +
     localLateral01 * yawDeg * 0.45f) * weight;

GameBootstrapper.InitializeCoreServicesPhaseAsync() initializes core, then calls WarmObjectPoolPresetsAsync(ct). Crater profile translation now exposed as:

public static float CraterHeightFuncSE(float normalizedRadius, float radius, float depth, float rimHeight, float floorFlatness, float halo)
{
    float x = math.max(0f, normalizedRadius);
    float floor = -depth * (1f - SmoothStep(0f, floorFlatness, x));
    float innerWall = -depth * (1f - SmoothStep(floorFlatness, 0.82f, x));
    float rim = rimHeight * math.exp(-64f * (x - 1f) * (x - 1f));
    float outer = rimHeight * 0.28f * math.exp(-8f * math.max(0f, x - 1f));
    float ejecta = halo * rimHeight * math.exp(-2.75f * math.max(0f, x - 1f));
    return math.select(floor, innerWall + rim, x >= floorFlatness) + outer + ejecta;
}
In-game result
Designers now have the requested MapMagic entry points: RidgedTerrain, CraterKernel, RiftFissure. Runtime seed path remains deterministic through GlobalRegistry.WorldSeedProvider; no UnityEngine.Random usage was found in the SpaceEngine terrain domain.

Spring-Damper Math

omega = 2π * springFrequencyHz * sqrt(max(0.1, splineResponseSharpness) / 10)
stiffness = omega * omega
damping = 2 * dampingRatio * omega
acceleration = ((target - position) * stiffness) - (velocity * damping)
velocity += acceleration * dt
position += velocity * dt
velocity = clamp_length(velocity, springMaxVelocity)
Boid Curtain HLSL

toPlayer = SafeNormalize(playerPositionCR - positionCR);
boidForward = SafeNormalize(velocityCR);
facingGate = smoothstep(0.12, 0.86, dot(boidForward, toPlayer));
lateral = SafeNormalize(cross(float3(0,1,0), toPlayer)) * sideSign;
acceleration += lateral * facingGate * playerThreatGate * max(0.18, panicFromPlayer) * max(_PanicWeight, 1.0) * 2.65;

Burst splat kernel core:

float rock = math.smoothstep(threshold - blend, threshold + blend, slopeDegrees);
float cavity = math.saturate((neighborAverage - center) * heightScale * CavityStrength);
float sediment = math.saturate(ReadSediment(index) * SedimentStrength);
float silt = math.saturate(math.max(cavity, sediment) * (1f - rock));
float sand = math.saturate((1f - rock) * (1f - silt));
Weights[index] = new float4(sand / total, rock / total, silt / total, cavity);

What was wrong: MapMagic normals were still gradient-derived in the bridge, and scatter rotation could fall back to yaw-only placement. Horizon fade also blended into fog without dithering.

What I did:

MapMagicBridge.TryGetNormal now resolves the cached MapMagic terrain tile and uses TerrainData.GetInterpolatedNormal, with gradient fallback only if the terrain normal is unavailable.
WorldProceduralScatterDirector now snaps flora placements to exact MapMagic height/normal first, clamps tilt to 30 degrees, and feeds that rotation into both pooled objects and GPUI Matrix4x4.TRS.
TerrainMaster.shader now applies interleaved-gradient dither to the planetary fade, blending into the noir floor color without clip/discard.

Bulkhead Slide Code

private void AdvanceBulkheadSlide(float deltaTime)
{
    CaptureBulkheadPose();
    if (emergencyBulkheadDoorMesh == null)
        return;

    float previousSlide = _bulkheadSlide01;
    float duration = Mathf.Max(0.01f, emergencyBulkheadSlideDurationSeconds);
    float step = Mathf.Max(0f, deltaTime) / duration;
    _bulkheadSlide01 = Mathf.MoveTowards(_bulkheadSlide01, _bulkheadSlideTarget01, step);
    if (Mathf.Approximately(previousSlide, _bulkheadSlide01))
        return;

    float eased = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, _bulkheadSlide01);
    emergencyBulkheadDoorMesh.localPosition = Vector3.LerpUnclamped(
        _bulkheadOpenLocalPosition,
        _bulkheadClosedLocalPosition,
        eased);

    if (_bulkheadSlideTarget01 < 1f || _bulkheadSlide01 < 0.999f || _bulkheadClangPlayed)
        return;

    _bulkheadClangPlayed = true;
    if (emergencyBulkheadClangSound != null && GlobalRegistry.Audio != null)
        GlobalRegistry.Audio.PlayAtPoint(emergencyBulkheadClangSound, _cachedTransform.position, 1f, 0.92f);
}

Waterline Shader Code
Core flooded-room visual path now tints, darkens, smooths, and warps UVs below the waterline:

float2 ResolveModuleWaterlineWarp(float3 positionWS, half submerged01)
{
    if (submerged01 <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    float2 rippleUv = positionWS.xz * 1.9 + _Time.y * float2(0.06, -0.041);
    half rippleX = (half)HectonCoreLitValueNoise2(rippleUv);
    half rippleY = (half)HectonCoreLitValueNoise2(rippleUv * 1.37 + float2(2.13, -0.71));
    return float2(rippleX - 0.5h, rippleY - 0.5h) * _WaterlineRefractionStrength * submerged01;
}

void ApplyModuleWaterline(half level01, half submerged01, inout half3 albedo, inout half smoothness)
{
    half floodStrength = saturate(level01 * submerged01);
    half tintStrength = floodStrength * (half)saturate(_WaterlineTint.a);
    half3 waterTint = half3(_WaterlineTint.r, _WaterlineTint.g, _WaterlineTint.b);
    albedo = saturate(albedo * (1.0h - submerged01 * (half)saturate(_WaterlineDarken) * 0.55h));
    albedo = lerp(albedo, waterTint, tintStrength);
    smoothness = lerp(smoothness, 0.88h, saturate(submerged01 * 0.62h));
}

4. METEOR SHOWER WATER IMPACTS:
- Action: Meteors shouldn't just hit the bottom SDF.
- Execution: If a meteor from RandomEventSystem intersects the Y=0 water plane, trigger a massive splash VFX and a rippling displacement wave in the water compute shader.
5. GAS GIANT RING CAUSTICS:
- Action: The rings must cast striped shadows.
- Execution: Calculate the ray-plane intersection for the Gas Giant's rings. Cast these distinct, striped shadows down through the water volume, creating massive, slow-moving bands of darkness in the shallow biomes.
6. ZERO-GC SUN TRACKING:
- Action: Sun movement must be mathematically pure.
- Execution: Ensure the rotation of the directional light and the updating of _SunDirection in global shader variables allocates 0 bytes. Use a fixed Matrix4x4 rotation calculation in HectonAtmosphereManager.

Math

HLSL Raymarch Core

const int steps = 8;
float noise = ResolveBlueNoise(screenUV);
float jitter = lerp(0.5, noise, _HectonShaftBlueNoiseJitter);

for (int i = 0; i < steps; i++)
{
    float travel = min(rayLength, (i + jitter) * stepLength);
    float3 p = cameraPositionWS + rayDirectionWS * travel;

    half3 scattering =
        EvaluateHeadlightScattering(p, rayDirectionWS) +
        EvaluateFlashlightScattering(p, rayDirectionWS);

    accumulated += scattering * siltField * caveFogFade * extinction * stepLength;
}
In EvaluateFlashlightScattering, voxel SDF shadows are optional; if no voxel volume exists, the cone still renders with shadow = 1.0.

    accumulated += EvaluateFlashlightScattering(samplePositionWS, rayDirectionWS)
        * stepLength * extinction;

- Action: Tools must sound dangerous.
- Execution: When using the Propulsion Tool or Laser Cutter, generate a low-frequency hum. If the tool overheats or cavitates, inject harsh, high-frequency white noise bursts into the DSP chain.

- Action: The PDA isn't a magical screen; it's hardware.
- Execution: Every UI tab switch, item craft, or scanner ping must trigger a localized, physical-sounding "click" or "clack" routed through the SpatialAudioManager at the PDA's 3D position in the player's hand.
5. DEPTH-BASED WATER CRUSH:
- Action: The ocean sounds different at 5km.
- Execution: As depth increases past 2000m, introduce a slow, sweeping Phaser/Flanger effect to the global ambient track. The deep ocean must sound "heavy" and distorted by pressure.
6. IMPACT MATERIAL THUDS:
- Action: Bumping into things must hurt.
- Execution: When PhysicsApplySystem registers a collision > 5m/s, play a procedural impact sound. Scale the amplitude by kinetic energy, and filter the tone based on the target's AudioMaterialID (Rock = dull thud, Metal = ringing clang).[III. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]

stageOut = -a * x + z;
z = x + a * stageOut;

output = -coefficient * input + delayed;
state = input + coefficient * output;

// four cascaded stages, then wet mix
wet = 0.38f * pressureDrive;
